// ---------- RUTA DE APRENDIZAJE ----------
function renderLearning() {
  const u = DATA.units.find(x => x.id === ST.unit) || DATA.units[1];
  const col = UCOLORS[u.id];
  const best = ST.pruebin[u.id];
  const read = ST.read[u.id] || {};
  const nread = Object.keys(read).length;
  const side = DATA.units.map(x => {
    const on = x.id === u.id, sc = ST.pruebin[x.id], ok = (sc || 0) >= PASS_PRUEBIN;
    return `<button onclick="go('learning',{unit:${x.id}})" class="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-left transition ${on ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800'}">
      <span class="w-9 h-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold ${on ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-800 text-' + UCOLORS[x.id] + '-300'}">${x.num}</span>
      <span class="text-sm font-semibold leading-tight flex-1">${esc(x.titulo)}</span>
      ${ok ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-400"></i>' : sc !== undefined ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-half-stroke text-amber-400"></i>' : ''}</button>`;
  }).join('');
  const idx = u.temas.map((t, i) => `<a href="#tema-${i}" onclick="event.preventDefault();document.getElementById('tema-${i}').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm py-1 ${read[i] ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-400'} hover:text-white"><i class="fa-solid ${read[i] ? 'fa-check' : 'fa-circle'} text-[8px] w-3"></i>${esc(t.titulo)}</a>`).join('');
  const temas = u.temas.map((t, i) => `
    <article id="tema-${i}" class="scroll-mt-24 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">

        <button onclick="toggleRead(${u.id},${i})" class="shrink-0 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border ${read[i] ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300' : 'border-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'}"><i class="fa-solid ${read[i] ? 'fa-check' : 'fa-bookmark'} mr-1"></i>${read[i] ? 'Leído' : 'Marcar leído'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div class="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-300 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info mr-1"></i>Primero: ¿qué es?</div><div class="text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">${md(t.idea || t.simple)}</div></div>
        <div class="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-300 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-bullseye mr-1"></i>¿Para qué sirve?</div><div class="text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">${md(t.uso || 'Te ayudará a escribir programas más claros y resolver tareas con datos.')}</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="rounded-2xl bg-${col}-500/5 border border-${col}-500/25 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-${col}-300 mb-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-ol mr-1"></i>Vamos paso a paso</div><ol class="space-y-2 text-slate-200">${(t.guia || []).map((p,j) => `<li class="flex gap-3"><span class="shrink-0 w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-${col}-500/15 text-${col}-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center">${j+1}</span><span>${inline(p)}</span></li>`).join('')}</ol></div>
      <div class="rounded-2xl bg-indigo-500/5 border border-indigo-500/20 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-300 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-lightbulb mr-1"></i>Una comparación fácil</div><div class="text-slate-200">${md(t.simple)}</div></div>
      <details class="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/60"><summary class="px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-slate-300 hover:text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-code mr-2 text-indigo-400"></i>Ver el detalle técnico y la sintaxis</summary><div class="px-5 pb-5 pt-1 text-slate-300 text-[15px] sm:text-base">${md(t.formal)}</div></details>
      ${t.codigo.map(c => `<div class="space-y-2">${codeBlock(c.c, c.t)}<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 px-1">Sigue el ejemplo de arriba hacia abajo. Identifica los datos, localiza la instrucción principal y predice el resultado antes de ejecutar el programa. Luego cambia un valor y observa qué sucede.</p></div>`).join('')}
      ${t.errores.length || t.tip ? `<div class="grid ${t.errores.length && t.tip ? 'md:grid-cols-2' : ''} gap-4">
        ${t.errores.length ? `<div class="rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/5 border border-rose-500/25 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-300 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-bug mr-1"></i>Errores comunes</div><ul class="space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-300">${t.errores.map(e => `<li class="flex gap-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark text-rose-400 mt-1"></i><span>${inline(e)}</span></li>`).join('')}</ul></div>` : ''}
        ${t.tip ? `<div class="rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/25 p-5"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-star mr-1"></i>Consejo para practicar</div><p class="text-sm text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">${inline(t.tip)}</p></div>` : ''}
      </div>` : ''}
    </article>`).join('');
  const retos = u.retos.map((r, i) => `
    <div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3">
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">${badge(r.nivel, r.nivel === 'Guiado' ? 'emerald' : r.nivel === 'Intermedio' ? 'amber' : 'rose', 'fa-flag')}<h4 class="font-bold text-white">${esc(r.titulo)}</h4></div>
      ${md(r.enunciado)}
      <div id="pasos-${u.id}-${i}" class="space-y-2"></div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <button onclick="nextStep(${u.id},${i})" id="bstep-${u.id}-${i}" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200"><i class="fa-solid fa-shoe-prints mr-1"></i>Ver siguiente paso (0/${r.pasos.length})</button>
        <button onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('hidden')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600/20 hover:bg-indigo-600/30 text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-200"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>Ver / ocultar solución</button>
        <div class="hidden w-full">${codeBlock(r.sol, 'Solución: ' + r.titulo)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>`).join('');
  const nEx = DATA.exercises.filter(e => e.u === u.id).length;
  $('#view-learning').innerHTML = `
  <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-[290px_1fr] gap-6 items-start">
    <aside class="lg:sticky lg:top-24 space-y-4">
      <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-3 space-y-1 max-h-[46vh] lg:max-h-[52vh] overflow-y-auto">${side}</div>
      <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-4 hidden lg:block"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 mb-2">En esta unidad</div>${idx}
        <a href="#retos" onclick="event.preventDefault();document.getElementById('retos').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'})" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm py-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-flag text-[10px] w-3"></i>Prácticas guiadas</a>
        <a href="#resumen" onclick="event.preventDefault();document.getElementById('resumen').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'})" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm py-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-note-sticky text-[10px] w-3"></i>Resumen</a>
      </div>
    </aside>
    <div class="space-y-6 min-w-0">
      <div class="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 to-slate-900/40 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-5">
        <div class="absolute -right-6 -top-10 text-[160px] font-extrabold text-${col}-500/10 select-none leading-none">${u.num}</div>
        <div class="relative flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">${badge(u.id === 0 ? 'Repaso previo' : 'Unidad ' + u.num, col, 'fa-solid ' + u.icono)}${badge(u.temas.length + ' temas', 'slate')}${badge(u.preguntas.length + ' preguntas en el banco', 'violet', 'fa-shuffle')}${best !== undefined ? badge('Mejor comprobación: ' + Math.round(best * 100) + '%', best >= PASS_PRUEBIN ? 'emerald' : 'amber', 'fa-trophy') : ''}</div>
        <div class="relative"><h2 class="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-white">${esc(u.titulo)}</h2><p class="text-${col}-300 font-semibold mt-1">${esc(u.subtitulo)}</p></div>
        <div class="relative text-slate-300">${md(u.intro)}</div>
        <div class="relative grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">${u.objetivos.map(o => `<div class="flex gap-2 text-sm text-slate-300"><i class="fa-solid fa-bullseye text-${col}-400 mt-1"></i><span>${inline(o)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>
        <div class="relative flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-2">
          <button onclick="openPruebin(${u.id})" class="px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 hover:from-indigo-500 hover:to-violet-500 text-white font-bold shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/30"><i class="fa-solid fa-shuffle mr-2"></i>Comprobación de la unidad (${PRUEBIN_N} al azar)</button>
          ${nEx ? `<button onclick="go('exercises',{unit:${u.id}})" class="px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-dumbbell mr-2 text-amber-400"></i>${nEx} ejercicios de esta unidad</button>` : ''}
          <span class="self-center text-sm text-slate-500">${nread}/${u.temas.length} temas leídos</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${temas}
      <section id="retos" class="scroll-mt-24 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 space-y-4">
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-flag text-${col}-400 mr-2"></i>Prácticas guiadas</h3>
        <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm">Intenta cada reto antes de mirar. Si te trancas, pide un paso a la vez.</p>
        ${retos}
      </section>
      <section id="resumen" class="scroll-mt-24 bg-gradient-to-br from-${col}-500/10 to-slate-900 border border-${col}-500/25 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 space-y-4">
        <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-note-sticky text-${col}-300 mr-2"></i>Lo esencial de esta unidad</h3>
        <ul class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">${u.resumen.map(r => `<li class="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-200">${inline(r)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-2">
          <button onclick="openPruebin(${u.id})" class="px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-shuffle mr-2"></i>Hacer el comprobación</button>
          ${u.id > 0 ? `<button onclick="go('learning',{unit:${u.id - 1}})" class="px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-2"></i>${esc(unitName(u.id - 1))}</button>` : ''}
          ${u.id < 9 ? `<button onclick="go('learning',{unit:${u.id + 1}})" class="px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-semibold">${esc(unitName(u.id + 1))}<i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right ml-2"></i></button>` : ''}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
function toggleRead(uid, i) { ST.read[uid] = ST.read[uid] || {}; if (ST.read[uid][i]) delete ST.read[uid][i]; else ST.read[uid][i] = 1; save(); const y = window.scrollY; renderLearning(); window.scrollTo(0, y); }
const STEPS = {};
function nextStep(uid, i) {
  const r = DATA.units.find(x => x.id === uid).retos[i], k = uid + '-' + i;
  STEPS[k] = Math.min((STEPS[k] || 0) + 1, r.pasos.length);
  $('#pasos-' + k).innerHTML = r.pasos.slice(0, STEPS[k]).map((p, j) => `<div class="fade-in flex gap-3 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300"><b class="text-indigo-300">Paso ${j + 1}</b><span>${inline(p)}</span></div>`).join('');
  $('#bstep-' + k).innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-shoe-prints mr-1"></i>${STEPS[k] < r.pasos.length ? 'Ver siguiente paso' : 'Todos los pasos'} (${STEPS[k]}/${r.pasos.length})`;
}

// ---------- MOTOR DE EVALUACIÓN ----------
// modo: 'pruebin' (retroalimentación inmediata), 'quiz' y 'sim' (retroalimentación al final)
let EV = null, TIMER = null;
function prepare(qs) { return qs.map(q => ({ q: q.q, code: q.code, e: q.e, opts: shuffle(q.o.map((t, i) => ({ t, ok: i === 0 }))) })); }
function openModal() { const m = $('#modal'); m.classList.remove('hidden'); m.classList.add('flex'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; }
function closeModal(force) {
  if (!force && EV && !EV.done && EV.answers.some(a => a !== null) && !confirm('¿Salir? Perderás este intento.')) return;
  $('#modal').classList.add('hidden'); $('#modal').classList.remove('flex'); document.body.style.overflow = ''; clearInterval(TIMER); EV = null;
}
function openPruebin(uid) {
  const u = DATA.units.find(x => x.id === uid);
  EV = { mode: 'pruebin', key: uid, title: 'Comprobación · ' + (u.id === 0 ? 'Repaso' : 'Unidad ' + u.num), sub: u.titulo, qs: prepare(shuffle(u.preguntas).slice(0, PRUEBIN_N)), i: 0, answers: [], pass: PASS_PRUEBIN };
  EV.answers = EV.qs.map(() => null); openModal(); renderQ();
}
function openQuiz(qid) {
  const qz = DATA.quizzes.find(x => x.id === qid);
  EV = { mode: 'quiz', key: qid, title: qz.titulo, sub: 'Temas: ' + qz.unidades.map(unitShort).join(', '), qs: prepare(shuffle(qz.pool).slice(0, QUIZ_N)), i: 0, pass: PASS_QUIZ };
  EV.answers = EV.qs.map(() => null); openModal(); renderQ();
}
function openSim(sid) {
  const s = DATA.sims.find(x => x.id === sid);
  let pool = [];
  s.unidades.forEach(uid => pool.push(...DATA.units.find(u => u.id === uid).preguntas));
  DATA.quizzes.filter(q => q.unidades.some(x => s.unidades.includes(x))).forEach(q => pool.push(...q.pool.filter(p => p.code)));
  EV = { mode: 'sim', key: sid, title: s.titulo, sub: s.desc, qs: prepare(shuffle(pool).slice(0, SIM_N)), i: 0, pass: 0.7, end: Date.now() + s.minutos * 60000 };
  EV.answers = EV.qs.map(() => null); openModal(); renderQ();
  clearInterval(TIMER); TIMER = setInterval(() => { if (!EV) return; const left = EV.end - Date.now(); const el = $('#ev-timer'); if (el) el.textContent = fmtTime(left); if (left <= 0) { clearInterval(TIMER); toast('Tiempo agotado', 'Se cerró el repaso.', 'warn'); finishEV(); } }, 1000);
}
const fmtTime = ms => { ms = Math.max(0, ms); const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000); return String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0'); };
function renderQ() {
  const q = EV.qs[EV.i], n = EV.qs.length, a = EV.answers[EV.i], instant = EV.mode === 'pruebin';
  const answered = EV.answers.filter(x => x !== null).length;
  const showFb = instant && a !== null;
  $('#modal-body').innerHTML = `
    <div class="space-y-5">
      <div class="pr-10"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-300">${esc(EV.title)}</div><div class="text-sm text-slate-400">${esc(EV.sub)}</div></div>
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="flex-1 h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-800 overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-indigo-500 transition-all" style="width:${100 * (EV.i + 1) / n}%"></div></div><span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">${EV.i + 1}/${n}</span>${EV.mode === 'sim' ? `<span class="text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300"><i class="fa-regular fa-clock mr-1"></i><span id="ev-timer">${fmtTime(EV.end - Date.now())}</span></span>` : ''}</div>
      <h3 class="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-white leading-snug">${inline(q.q)}</h3>
      ${q.code ? codeBlock(q.code, 'Código') : ''}
      <div class="space-y-2.5">
        ${q.opts.map((o, j) => {
          let cls = 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 hover:border-indigo-400 text-slate-200';
          if (a === j && !showFb) cls = 'border-indigo-400 bg-indigo-500/15 text-white';
          if (showFb) cls = o.ok ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-100' : a === j ? 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-100' : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-500';
          return `<button ${showFb ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="pick(${j})" class="opt w-full text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl border ${cls} flex gap-3 items-start"><span class="w-7 h-7 shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center">${'ABCD'[j]}</span><span class="pt-0.5 text-[15px]">${inline(o.t)}</span>${showFb && o.ok ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-check ml-auto text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i>' : ''}</button>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      ${showFb ? `<div class="fade-in rounded-2xl p-4 border ${q.opts[a].ok ? 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5' : 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/5'}"><div class="font-bold ${q.opts[a].ok ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-300'} mb-1">${q.opts[a].ok ? '¡Correcto!' : 'Incorrecto'}</div><p class="text-sm text-slate-300">${inline(q.e)}</p></div>` : ''}
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-2">
        <button onclick="moveQ(-1)" ${EV.i === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''} class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-30"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-1"></i>Anterior</button>
        <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${answered} de ${n} respondidas</span>
        ${EV.i < n - 1 ? `<button onclick="moveQ(1)" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-sm font-bold">Siguiente<i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right ml-1"></i></button>`
          : `<button onclick="finishEV()" class="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-sm font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-flag-checkered mr-1"></i>Terminar</button>`}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}
function pick(j) { if (EV.mode === 'pruebin' && EV.answers[EV.i] !== null) return; EV.answers[EV.i] = j; renderQ(); }
function moveQ(d) { EV.i = Math.max(0, Math.min(EV.qs.length - 1, EV.i + d)); renderQ(); $('#modal-body').scrollTop = 0; }
function finishEV() {
  const blank = EV.answers.filter(a => a === null).length;
  if (blank && EV.mode !== 'sim' && !confirm(`Tienes ${blank} sin responder (cuentan como incorrectas). ¿Terminar?`)) return;
  if (blank && EV.mode === 'sim' && EV.end > Date.now() && !confirm(`Tienes ${blank} sin responder. ¿Entregar el simulacro?`)) return;
  clearInterval(TIMER); EV.done = true;
  const ok = EV.qs.filter((q, i) => EV.answers[i] !== null && q.opts[EV.answers[i]].ok).length, n = EV.qs.length, sc = ok / n, passed = sc >= EV.pass;
  if (EV.mode === 'pruebin') { ST.pruebin[EV.key] = Math.max(ST.pruebin[EV.key] || 0, sc); }
  if (EV.mode === 'quiz') { const p = ST.quiz[EV.key] || { best: 0, intentos: 0, passed: false }; p.best = Math.max(p.best, sc); p.intentos++; p.passed = p.passed || passed; ST.quiz[EV.key] = p; }
  if (EV.mode === 'sim') { ST.sim[EV.key] = Math.max(ST.sim[EV.key] || 0, sc); }
  save();
  const wrong = EV.qs.map((q, i) => ({ q, a: EV.answers[i] })).filter(x => x.a === null || !x.q.opts[x.a].ok);
  const isQuiz = EV.mode === 'quiz';
  $('#modal-body').innerHTML = `
    <div class="space-y-6 fade-in">
      <div class="text-center space-y-3 pt-2">
        <div class="w-20 h-20 mx-auto rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center text-4xl ${passed ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400'}"><i class="fa-solid ${passed ? 'fa-trophy' : 'fa-rotate-right'}"></i></div>
        <div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">${esc(EV.title)}</div>
        <div class="text-5xl font-extrabold text-white">${ok}/${n} <span class="text-2xl text-slate-400">(${Math.round(sc * 100)}%)</span></div>
        <p class="text-slate-300">${passed ? (isQuiz ? '¡Aprobado! Este quiz queda superado.' : '¡Muy bien! Dominas este bloque.') : isQuiz ? `Necesitas ${Math.round(PASS_QUIZ * 100)}% para pasar. Repite: tendrás preguntas nuevas y en otro orden.` : `Repasa los temas de las preguntas falladas y vuelve a intentarlo: saldrán preguntas distintas.`}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
        <button onclick="retryEV()" class="px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl ${passed ? 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700' : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500'} font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-shuffle mr-2"></i>${passed ? 'Practicar otra vez' : 'Reintentar con preguntas nuevas'}</button>
        <button onclick="closeModal(true);rerender()" class="px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 font-semibold">Cerrar</button>
      </div>
      ${wrong.length ? `<div class="space-y-3"><h4 class="font-bold text-white">Revisión de las ${wrong.length} que fallaste</h4>
        ${wrong.map(w => `<div class="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 space-y-2"><p class="font-semibold text-slate-100">${inline(w.q.q)}</p>${w.q.code ? `<pre class="c text-xs bg-[#0b1120] rounded-xl p-3 overflow-x-auto">${hl(w.q.code)}</pre>` : ''}
          ${w.a !== null ? `<p class="text-sm text-rose-300"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark mr-1"></i>Tu respuesta: ${inline(w.q.opts[w.a].t)}</p>` : '<p class="text-sm text-slate-500">Sin responder</p>'}
          <p class="text-sm text-emerald-300"><i class="fa-solid fa-check mr-1"></i>Correcta: ${inline(w.q.opts.find(o => o.ok).t)}</p><p class="text-sm text-slate-400">${inline(w.q.e)}</p></div>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
    </div>`;
  $('#modal-body').scrollTop = 0;
}
function retryEV() { const m = EV.mode, k = EV.key; EV = null; if (m === 'pruebin') openPruebin(k); else if (m === 'quiz') openQuiz(k); else openSim(k); }
function rerender() { if (ST.view === 'learning') { const y = scrollY; renderLearning(); scrollTo(0, y); } if (ST.view === 'quizzes') renderQuizzes(); if (ST.view === 'exams') renderExams(); if (ST.view === 'home') renderHome(); }

// ---------- EJERCICIOS ----------
const EXF = { u: 'all', n: 'all', q: '' };
function renderExercises() {
  const units = [['all', 'Todas'], ...DATA.units.map(u => [u.id, unitShort(u.id)]), [10, 'Proyectos']];
  const levels = [['all', 'Todos los niveles'], [1, 'Fácil'], [2, 'Intermedio'], [3, 'Avanzado'], [4, 'Proyecto']];
  $('#view-exercises').innerHTML = `
  <div class="space-y-6">
    <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-6">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-4"><div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-violet-600/20 text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl"><i class="fa-solid fa-dumbbell"></i></div>
        <div><h2 class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-white">Banco de ejercicios</h2><p class="text-slate-400">Actividades organizadas por tema y dificultad. Cada una indica qué conceptos puedes utilizar y ofrece una pista para avanzar.</p></div></div>
      <div class="space-y-3">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${units.map(u => `<button onclick="setEXF('u','${u[0]}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 ${String(EXF.u) === String(u[0]) ? 'chip-on' : ''}">${esc(u[1])}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">${levels.map(l => `<button onclick="setEXF('n','${l[0]}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 ${String(EXF.n) === String(l[0]) ? 'chip-on' : ''}">${esc(l[1])}</button>`).join('')}
          <div class="relative ml-auto w-full sm:w-72"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass absolute left-3 top-3 text-slate-500 text-sm"></i><input id="exq" value="${esc(EXF.q)}" oninput="EXF.q=this.value;renderExList()" placeholder="Buscar (ej. recursiva, matriz, BOOK)" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-400"></div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="ex-list" class="space-y-8"></div>
  </div>`;
  renderExList();
}
function setEXF(k, v) { EXF[k] = v === 'all' ? 'all' : Number(v); renderExercises(); }
function renderExList() {
  const q = EXF.q.trim().toLowerCase();
  let list = DATA.exercises.filter(e => (EXF.u === 'all' || e.u === EXF.u) && (EXF.n === 'all' || e.nivel === EXF.n) && (!q || (e.titulo + ' ' + e.enunciado + ' ' + e.usar.join(' ') + ' ' + (e.fuente || '')).toLowerCase().includes(q)));
  list.sort((a, b) => a.u - b.u || a.nivel - b.nivel || a.id - b.id);
  const groups = {}; list.forEach(e => (groups[e.u] = groups[e.u] || []).push(e));
  const html = Object.keys(groups).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b).map(u => {
    const head = u === 10 ? ['Proyectos prácticos', 'Programas completos para construir y explorar', 'fa-rocket', 'violet'] : [unitName(u) + ': ' + DATA.units[u].titulo, DATA.units[u].subtitulo, DATA.units[u].icono, UCOLORS[u]];
    return `<div class="space-y-3"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><span class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-${head[3]}-500/15 text-${head[3]}-300 flex items-center justify-center"><i class="fa-solid ${head[2]}"></i></span><div><h3 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white">${esc(head[0])}</h3><p class="text-sm text-slate-500">${esc(head[1])}</p></div></div>
      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${groups[u].map(exCard).join('')}</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  $('#ex-list').innerHTML = html || '<p class="text-center text-slate-500 py-10">Ningún ejercicio coincide con el filtro.</p>';
}
function exCard(e) {
  const [ln, lc] = LEVELS[e.nivel];
  return `<details class="group bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-700 open:border-indigo-500/40 open:md:col-span-2 transition">
    <summary class="p-5 flex items-start gap-3"><span class="text-xs font-mono text-slate-600 pt-1 w-8">#${e.id}</span>
      <div class="flex-1 space-y-2"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${badge(ln, lc)}${e.u !== 10 ? badge(unitShort(e.u), UCOLORS[e.u]) : ''}${e.sol ? badge('Con solución', 'indigo', 'fa-check') : ''}</div>
        <h4 class="font-bold text-white leading-snug">${esc(e.titulo)}</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Actividad de práctica</p></div>
      <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down text-slate-500 group-open:rotate-180 transition mt-1"></i></summary>
    <div class="px-5 pb-5 space-y-4 border-t border-slate-800 pt-4">
      <div class="text-slate-300 text-[15px]">${md(e.enunciado)}</div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 mr-1">Qué usar:</span>${e.usar.map(t => `<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-300 font-mono">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <button onclick="this.parentElement.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('hidden')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-200 text-sm font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-lightbulb mr-1"></i>Pista</button>
        ${e.sol ? `<button onclick="this.parentElement.parentElement.querySelector('.ex-sol').classList.toggle('hidden')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600/20 hover:bg-indigo-600/30 text-indigo-200 text-sm font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>Solución</button>` : ''}
        ${e.goto ? `<button onclick="${e.goto.v === 'exams' ? `go('exams',{exam:'${e.goto.exam || ''}'})` : `go('learning',{unit:${e.goto.unit}})`}" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-up-right-from-square mr-1"></i>${e.goto.v === 'exams' ? 'Ver desafíos' : 'Ir a la teoría'}</button>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="hidden rounded-xl bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/20 p-4 text-sm text-slate-200">${inline(e.pista)}</div>
      ${e.sol ? `<div class="ex-sol hidden">${codeBlock(e.sol, 'Solución #' + e.id)}</div>` : ''}
    </div></details>`;
}

// ---------- QUIZZES ----------
function renderQuizzes() {
  $('#view-quizzes').innerHTML = `
  <div class="space-y-6">
    <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-3">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-4"><div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl"><i class="fa-solid fa-bolt"></i></div>
        <div><h2 class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-white">Autoevaluaciones</h2><p class="text-slate-400">Pequeñas pruebas para comprobar si comprendiste los conceptos y decidir qué necesitas repasar.</p></div></div>
      <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-3 text-sm">
        <div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4"><b class="text-white">${QUIZ_N} preguntas</b><p class="text-slate-400">elegidas al azar de un banco de preguntas.</p></div>
        <div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4"><b class="text-white">Objetivo: ${Math.round(PASS_QUIZ * 100)}%</b><p class="text-slate-400">si no llegas, lo repites con preguntas nuevas y barajadas.</p></div>
        <div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4"><b class="text-white">Primero intenta resolverlo</b><p class="text-slate-400">la explicación de las respuestas aparece al terminar.</p></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
      ${DATA.quizzes.map(q => { const p = ST.quiz[q.id]; return `
        <div class="bg-slate-900 border ${p && p.passed ? 'border-emerald-500/40' : 'border-slate-800'} rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">Temas de práctica</span>${p ? (p.passed ? badge('Completada', 'emerald', 'fa-check') : badge('Pendiente', 'amber')) : badge('Sin intentar', 'slate')}</div>
          <h3 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">${esc(q.titulo)}</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 flex-1">${esc(q.desc)}</p>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${q.unidades.map(u => badge(unitShort(u), UCOLORS[u])).join('')}</div>
          ${p ? `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Mejor resultado: <b class="text-slate-200">${Math.round(p.best * 100)}%</b> · Intentos: ${p.intentos}</p>` : ''}
          <button onclick="openQuiz(${q.id})" class="w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-play mr-2"></i>${p ? 'Volver a practicar' : 'Comenzar práctica'}</button>
        </div>`; }).join('')}
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

// ---------- DESAFÍOS PRÁCTICOS ----------
const EXAMV = { tab: 'real', open: null };
let EXAM_T = null;
function renderExams() {
  const tabs = `<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
    <button onclick="EXAMV.tab='real';EXAMV.open=null;renderExams()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 ${EXAMV.tab === 'real' ? 'chip-on' : ''}"><i class="fa-solid fa-code mr-1"></i>Desafíos de código</button>
    <button onclick="EXAMV.tab='sim';EXAMV.open=null;renderExams()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 ${EXAMV.tab === 'sim' ? 'chip-on' : ''}"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-check mr-1"></i>Repasos de conceptos</button></div>`;
  let body = '';
  if (EXAMV.tab === 'sim') body = `
    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-5">${DATA.sims.map(s => { const b = ST.sim[s.id]; return `
      <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">${badge(s.minutos + ' min', 'amber', 'fa-clock')}${b !== undefined ? badge('Mejor: ' + Math.round(b * 100) + '%', b >= 0.7 ? 'emerald' : 'rose') : ''}</div>
        <h3 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white">${esc(s.titulo)}</h3><p class="text-sm text-slate-400 flex-1">${esc(s.desc)}</p>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${s.unidades.map(u => badge(unitShort(u), UCOLORS[u])).join('')}</div>
        <button onclick="openSim('${s.id}')" class="w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-stopwatch mr-2"></i>Comenzar repaso (${SIM_N} preguntas)</button>
      </div>`; }).join('')}</div>
    <p class="text-sm text-slate-500">Los simulacros teóricos mezclan preguntas de las autoevaluaciones y trazas de código de los quizzes. Sirven de repaso; los desafíos de programación son de programar, practícalos en la otra pestaña.</p>`;
  else if (!EXAMV.open) body = `
    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">${DATA.exams.map(x => {
      const done = x.problemas.filter((p, i) => (ST.code[x.id + i] || '').trim()).length;
      return `<button onclick="EXAMV.open='${x.id}';renderExams();scrollTo(0,0)" class="text-left bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 hover:border-amber-500/50 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-3 transition">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${badge(x.tipo, x.tipo.startsWith('Primer') ? 'indigo' : 'rose')}${badge(x.version, 'slate')}${badge(x.problemas.length + ' puntos', 'amber')}</div>
        <h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">${esc(x.titulo)}</h3>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">${x.unidades.map(u => `<span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-${UCOLORS[u]}-300">${unitShort(u)}</span>`).join('<span class="text-slate-700">·</span>')}</div>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">${done ? `Has escrito ${done} de ${x.problemas.length} respuestas` : 'Sin empezar'}</p></button>`; }).join('')}</div>`;
  else body = examDetail(DATA.exams.find(x => x.id === EXAMV.open));
  $('#view-exams').innerHTML = `
  <div class="space-y-6">
    <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-5">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-4"><div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-lines"></i></div>
        <div><h2 class="text-3xl font-extrabold text-white">Desafíos</h2><p class="text-slate-400">Resuelve problemas que combinan varios conceptos. Puedes escribir tu código, utilizar el temporizador como referencia, revisar una lista de comprobación y comparar tu solución con una propuesta explicada.</p></div></div>
      ${tabs}
    </div>
    ${body}
  </div>`;
  if (EXAMV.open && EXAMV.tab === 'real') startExamTicker();
}
function examDetail(x) {
  const t = ST['timer_' + x.id] || { left: x.minutos * 60000, run: false, at: 0 };
  return `
  <div class="space-y-6">
    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3"><button onclick="EXAMV.open=null;renderExams()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sm font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-1"></i>Volver a todos los desafíos</button></div>
    <div class="bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-5 shadow-2xl">
      <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start md:justify-between gap-4 border-b-2 border-slate-900 pb-4">
        <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold leading-tight uppercase"><div>C-Master Pro</div><div class="normal-case font-semibold mt-1">Desafío de programación en C</div></div>
        <div class="text-right"><div class="text-2xl font-extrabold">${esc(x.tipo)}</div><div class="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">${esc(x.version)}</div></div>
      </div>
      <ol class="list-decimal pl-5 text-sm font-bold space-y-1">${x.instrucciones.map(i => `<li>${esc(i).toUpperCase()}</li>`).join('')}</ol>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 no-print bg-slate-100 rounded-2xl p-3">
        <span class="font-mono text-2xl font-extrabold w-24" id="exam-clock">${fmtTime(t.run ? t.left - (Date.now() - t.at) : t.left)}</span>
        <button onclick="examTimer('${x.id}','toggle')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl ${t.run ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-slate-900'} text-white text-sm font-bold"><i class="fa-solid ${t.run ? 'fa-pause' : 'fa-play'} mr-1"></i>${t.run ? 'Pausar' : 'Iniciar temporizador'}</button>
        <button onclick="examTimer('${x.id}','reset')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-sm font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left mr-1"></i>Reiniciar</button>
        <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${x.minutos} minutos sugeridos · tus respuestas se guardan en este navegador</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${x.defs ? codeBlock(x.defs, 'Tipos y datos disponibles') : ''}
    ${x.problemas.map((p, i) => problemCard(x, p, i)).join('')}
  </div>`;
}
function problemCard(x, p, i) {
  const key = x.id + i, rub = ST.rub[key] || {}, nr = Object.keys(rub).filter(k => rub[k]).length;
  return `
  <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 space-y-5">
    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">${badge('Actividad ' + (i + 1), 'amber', 'fa-pen')}${p.unidades.map(u => `<button onclick="go('learning',{unit:${u}})">${badge(unitShort(u), UCOLORS[u])}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">${esc(p.titulo)}</h3>
    <div class="text-slate-300 text-[15px]">${md(p.enunciado)}</div>
    ${p.ejemplo ? `<div><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 mb-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-table mr-1"></i>Ejemplo de entrada y resultado</div><pre class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 text-[13px] text-slate-300 overflow-x-auto leading-relaxed">${esc(p.ejemplo)}</pre></div>` : ''}
    ${p.conocidas ? codeBlock(p.conocidas, 'Funciones disponibles para utilizar') : ''}
    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 mr-1">Qué practicarás:</span>${p.usar.map(t => `<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-300 font-mono">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="space-y-2">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><label class="text-sm font-bold text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-keyboard text-indigo-400 mr-1"></i>Tu solución</label><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">Escribe tu solución · Tab inserta sangría</span></div>
      <textarea spellcheck="false" oninput="ST.code['${key}']=this.value;save()" onkeydown="tabKey(event)" rows="14" class="code w-full bg-[#0b1120] border border-slate-700 focus:border-indigo-400 focus:outline-none rounded-2xl p-4 text-sm text-slate-200 leading-relaxed" placeholder="/* escribe aquí tu función */">${esc(ST.code[key] || '')}</textarea>
    </div>
    ${p.rubrica.length ? `<div class="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 space-y-2"><div class="flex justify-between"><span class="text-sm font-bold text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-check text-emerald-400 mr-1"></i>Autoevaluación con rúbrica</span><span class="text-sm font-bold ${nr === p.rubrica.length ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-400'}">${nr}/${p.rubrica.length}</span></div>
      ${p.rubrica.map((r, j) => `<label class="flex gap-3 text-sm text-slate-300 cursor-pointer"><input type="checkbox" ${rub[j] ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="setRub('${key}',${j},this.checked)" class="mt-1 accent-emerald-500"><span>${inline(r)}</span></label>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      <button onclick="revealSol(this)" class="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-sm font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>Ver una solución explicada</button>
    </div>
    <div class="hidden space-y-4">
      <div class="rounded-2xl bg-indigo-500/5 border border-indigo-500/25 p-5 text-slate-200"><div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-300 mb-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-chalkboard-user mr-1"></i>Una forma de resolverlo</div>${md(p.explicacion)}</div>
      ${codeBlock(p.solcode, 'Solución: ' + p.titulo)}
      ${p.full ? `<details class="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800"><summary class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white"><i class="fa-solid fa-flask mr-1 text-emerald-400"></i>Programa completo de prueba (con las funciones conocidas simuladas y un main con el ejemplo)</summary><div class="p-3">${codeBlock(p.full, 'Programa de prueba · compila en tu entorno de C')}</div></details>` : ''}
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
function revealSol(btn) {
  const box = btn.parentElement.nextElementSibling;
  if (box.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    const ta = btn.closest('.rounded-3xl').querySelector('textarea');
    if (!ta.value.trim() && !confirm('Todavía no has escrito tu solución. ¿Ver la respuesta de todos modos?')) return;
    box.classList.remove('hidden'); btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-eye-slash mr-1"></i>Ocultar explicación';
  } else { box.classList.add('hidden'); btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>Ver una solución explicada'; }
}
function setRub(key, j, v) { ST.rub[key] = ST.rub[key] || {}; ST.rub[key][j] = v; save(); const y = scrollY; renderExams(); scrollTo(0, y); }
function tabKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Tab') { e.preventDefault(); const t = e.target, s = t.selectionStart; t.value = t.value.slice(0, s) + '    ' + t.value.slice(t.selectionEnd); t.selectionStart = t.selectionEnd = s + 4; t.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); } }
function examTimer(id, act) {
  const x = DATA.exams.find(e => e.id === id), k = 'timer_' + id;
  let t = ST[k] || { left: x.minutos * 60000, run: false, at: 0 };
  if (act === 'toggle') { if (t.run) { t.left -= Date.now() - t.at; t.run = false; } else { t.run = true; t.at = Date.now(); } }
  if (act === 'reset') t = { left: x.minutos * 60000, run: false, at: 0 };
  ST[k] = t; save(); const y = scrollY; renderExams(); scrollTo(0, y);
}
function startExamTicker() {
  clearInterval(EXAM_T);
  EXAM_T = setInterval(() => {
    const id = EXAMV.open, el = $('#exam-clock'); if (!id || !el || ST.view !== 'exams') { clearInterval(EXAM_T); return; }
    const t = ST['timer_' + id]; if (!t || !t.run) return;
    const left = t.left - (Date.now() - t.at); el.textContent = fmtTime(left);
    if (left <= 0) { t.left = 0; t.run = false; save(); toast('¡Tiempo!', 'Terminó el tiempo sugerido. Revisa tus respuestas y vuelve a practicar.', 'warn'); renderExams(); }
  }, 1000);
}

// ---------- arranque ----------
function renderAll() { renderNav(); renderEnvironment(); go(ST.view || 'home', { unit: ST.unit }); }
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && !$('#modal').classList.contains('hidden')) closeModal(); });
renderAll();
</script>
</body>
</html>
